This includes creating new test suites, adding test cases to existing files, implementing test factories, setting up parameterized tests, or configuring mocks and stubs. The agent focuses exclusively on unit testing and should not be used for integration, e2e, or component testing.\n\nExamples:\n\n&lt;example&gt;\nContext: The user has just written a new utility function and needs unit tests.\nuser: "Write a function that validates email addresses"\nassistant: "Here is the email validation fun… ([milafrerichs/agents-skills-collection](https://github.com/milafrerichs/agents-skills-collection/blob/HEAD/agents/vitest-unit-tester.md))
- **vitest-unit-tester**: Writes and improves Vitest unit tests: new test suites, edge-case and error-path coverage, mocks, fakes and test utilities. Focuses on behavior, not implementation details. Not for end-to-end or browser tests. - **Vitor Visão**: 1. **Fórmula 6 elementos é inegociável** — Subject → Action → Environment → Camera → Style → Constraints. Nenhum prompt sai sem todos os 6. 2. **60 a 100 palavras por prompt** — Abaixo de 40 gera inconsistência. Acima de 120 confunde o modelo. 3. **Uma instrução de câmera por prompt** — Dois movimentos simultâneos produzem resultado errático. Escolher o mais importante. 4. **9:16 sempre incluído** ([workest-bootstrap-owner/Squads100](https://github.com/workest-bootstrap-owner/Squads100/blob/HEAD/squads/videos-animados-2/agents/vitor-visao.agent.md))
- **vitrine**: Desenha e atualiza as landing pages dos plugins do marketplace. Lê as fontes do plugin (README/CHANGELOG/manifest), cria pages/&lt;plugin&gt;/index.html com identidade própria e sela o hash (pages-guard stamp). Referenciado pelo block de merge.
